      "/wO9BMQE/wPEBAYE/wO6BL0EtwS6BP8DsAS3BP0D+wOnBLAE9AOnBPsD9wOnBPQD7wOnBPcD7wOkBKcEWAOkBO8D7AOkBFgD5wNXA+wD4AOaBFcD4AOWBJoE" +
      "AwR9BHEE",
    };

    const catSurfaceMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      vertexColors: true, roughness: .82, metalness: 0, flatShading: false
    });

    function decodeBase64Bytes(encoded) {
      const raw = atob(encoded);
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(raw.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) bytes[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i);
      return bytes;
    }

    function decodeTypedArray(encoded, Type) {
      const bytes = decodeBase64Bytes(encoded);
      const copy = new Uint8Array(bytes.byteLength);
      copy.set(bytes);
      return new Type(copy.buffer);
    }

    function buildEmbeddedCatGeometry(data) {
      const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(decodeTypedArray(data.positions, Float32Array), 3));
      geometry.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(decodeTypedArray(data.normals, Float32Array), 3));
      geometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(decodeBase64Bytes(data.colors), 3, true));
      const IndexType = data.index32 ? Uint32Array : Uint16Array;
      geometry.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(decodeTypedArray(data.indices, IndexType), 1));
      geometry.computeBoundingBox();
      geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
      return geometry;
    }

    const furPalette = [0xe19a5b, 0x44464f, 0xc9b9a3, 0xe8dfce, 0x7e6758, 0x9a9ca2];

    const boykisserFullGeometry = buildEmbeddedCatGeometry(BOYKISSER_FULL);
    const boykisserLodGeometry = buildEmbeddedCatGeometry(BOYKISSER_LOD);

    function createBoykisserMesh(geometry = boykisserFullGeometry, castShadow = true) {
      const model = mesh(geometry, catSurfaceMaterial, castShadow, false);
      model.frustumCulled = true;
      return model;
    }

    // Same imported cat asset is now used for every character and cat weapon.
    // Geometry and material are shared, so replacement does not multiply GPU memory.
    function createCat({scale = 1, geometry = boykisserFullGeometry, castShadow = false} = {}) {
      const root = new THREE.Group();
      const model = createBoykisserMesh(geometry, castShadow);
      model.scale.setScalar(2.75); // source mesh is normalized to one unit high
      model.updateMatrix();model.matrixAutoUpdate=false;
      root.add(model);
      root.scale.setScalar(scale);
      root.userData.model = model;
      return root;
    }

    function createGunCat(_fur, type, geometry = boykisserFullGeometry) {
      const root = new THREE.Group();
      const model = createBoykisserMesh(geometry, false);
      // Lay the upright model along the firing axis. Its head points toward -Z
      // while its face points upward, preserving the weaponized-cat silhouette.
      model.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      const width = type === 'shotgun' ? 2.58 : type === 'smg' ? 2.08 : 2.3;
      const length = type === 'smg' ? 2.12 : 2.3;
      model.scale.set(width, length, width);
      model.position.set(0, -.08, .66);
      model.updateMatrix();model.matrixAutoUpdate=false;
      root.add(model);
      root.userData.model = model;
      return root;
    }

    // ---------- Game state ----------
    const player = {
      pos: new THREE.Vector3(-47, 1.65, 0),
      velY: 0, velX:0, velZ:0, moveSpeed:0, crouched:false, yaw: -Math.PI / 2, pitch: 0,
      health: 100, dead: false, radius: .48,
      team: 'cats', spawnSlot: 2, invulnerableUntil: 0,
      kills: 0, deaths: 0, onGround: true, bob: 0
    };
    player.money = 800;
    player.gunGameLevel = 0;
    player.gunGameKillsOnLevel = 0;

    // Death bodies reuse CatStrike's existing shared cat geometry/material instead of
    // allocating duplicate textures or a second character asset. Bots tumble their
    // own live root; the first-person player needs one dedicated world-space body.
    const playerRagdoll = createCat({scale:.72,geometry:boykisserLodGeometry,castShadow:false});
    playerRagdoll.name='CatStrike.PlayerRagdoll';
    playerRagdoll.visible=false;
    playerRagdoll.matrixAutoUpdate=false;
    scene.add(playerRagdoll);
    const playerRagdollState={active:false,age:0,velX:0,velY:0,velZ:0,targetX:0,targetZ:0,deathYaw:0};

    // R179 first-death verification pass. A focused Chromium benchmark of the
    // actual fatal JS/DOM/WebAudio path measured ~1.2 ms on its first execution,
    // so the heavy hitch is render-side rather than ragdoll/audio bookkeeping.
    // Warm two distinct GPU paths while the loading overlay still covers the canvas:
    // (1) one all-visible upload render, then (2) the *real* third-person death-camera
    // transition with normal object/BatchedMesh culling enabled. R178 only did (1),
    // which skipped the exact view-dependent multi-draw/culling state used on death.
    let deathRenderPathWarmed=false;
    function warmDeathRenderPath(){
      if(deathRenderPathWarmed)return true;
      deathRenderPathWarmed=true;
      const savedTarget=renderer.getRenderTarget();
      const savedRagdollVisible=playerRagdoll.visible;
      const savedRagdollPosition=playerRagdoll.position.clone();
      const savedRagdollQuaternion=playerRagdoll.quaternion.clone();
      const savedCameraPosition=camera.position.clone();
      const savedCameraQuaternion=camera.quaternion.clone();
      const savedCull=[];
      const savedBatchedCull=[];
      let warmTarget=null;
      try{
        playerRagdoll.position.set(player.pos.x,.03,player.pos.z);
        playerRagdoll.rotation.set(0,player.yaw,0);
        playerRagdoll.visible=true;playerRagdoll.updateMatrix();
        warmTarget=new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(8,8,{depthBuffer:true,stencilBuffer:false});
        warmTarget.texture.generateMipmaps=false;
        warmTarget.texture.minFilter=THREE.NearestFilter;warmTarget.texture.magFilter=THREE.NearestFilter;
        renderer.setRenderTarget(warmTarget);

        // Phase 1: force every currently-visible drawable through WebGL once so
        // buffers/VAOs and shared materials cannot first-upload on the fatal frame.
        scene.traverse(object=>{
          if(!object.visible)return;
          if(object.isMesh||object.isLine||object.isPoints){savedCull.push([object,object.frustumCulled]);object.frustumCulled=false;}
          if(object.isBatchedMesh&&'perObjectFrustumCulled' in object){savedBatchedCull.push([object,object.perObjectFrustumCulled]);object.perObjectFrustumCulled=false;}
        });
        renderer.render(scene,camera);

        // Phase 2: restore normal culling, then execute the same camera movement
        // used by updateRagdolls(). This is the path R178 did not exercise.
        for(let i=savedBatchedCull.length-1;i>=0;i--){const [object,value]=savedBatchedCull[i];object.perObjectFrustumCulled=value;}
        for(let i=savedCull.length-1;i>=0;i--){const [object,value]=savedCull[i];object.frustumCulled=value;}
        savedBatchedCull.length=0;savedCull.length=0;
        const target=new THREE.Vector3(player.pos.x,.78,player.pos.z);
        const desired=new THREE.Vector3(player.pos.x+Math.sin(player.yaw)*3.4,2.65,player.pos.z+Math.cos(player.yaw)*3.4);
        camera.position.copy(savedCameraPosition);camera.quaternion.copy(savedCameraQuaternion);
        for(let frame=0;frame<12;frame++){
          camera.position.lerp(desired,1-Math.exp(-6.5/60));camera.lookAt(target);
          renderer.render(scene,camera);
        }
        // Drain deferred driver work here, before gameplay begins.
        renderer.getContext().finish();
        return true;
      }catch(error){safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] death-path GPU warmup fallback:',error);return false;}
      finally{
        for(let i=savedBatchedCull.length-1;i>=0;i--){const [object,value]=savedBatchedCull[i];object.perObjectFrustumCulled=value;}
        for(let i=savedCull.length-1;i>=0;i--){const [object,value]=savedCull[i];object.frustumCulled=value;}
        playerRagdoll.visible=savedRagdollVisible;
        playerRagdoll.position.copy(savedRagdollPosition);playerRagdoll.quaternion.copy(savedRagdollQuaternion);playerRagdoll.updateMatrix();
        camera.position.copy(savedCameraPosition);camera.quaternion.copy(savedCameraQuaternion);camera.updateMatrixWorld(true);
        renderer.setRenderTarget(savedTarget);
        warmTarget?.dispose();
      }
    }
    const teamKills = { cats: 0, meowfia: 0 };
    camera.position.copy(player.pos);
    camera.rotation.set(player.pitch, player.yaw, 0, 'YXZ');
    // Cache the exact Euler values already applied to the camera. During normal
    // play, aim often stays unchanged for many frames; avoid re-running Three.js'
    // Euler -> quaternion synchronization until pitch/yaw actually changes.
    // NaN intentionally remains a repeated-update case and signed zero changes
    // are distinguished so edge-case behavior matches the unconditional path.
    let lastCameraPitch=player.pitch,lastCameraYaw=player.yaw;

    const ACCURACY_CLASS = {
      pistol:{base:.0017,move:.024,air:.075,spray:.0012,recover:.045,recoilPitch:.009,recoilYaw:.0055},
      smg:{base:.0026,move:.016,air:.055,spray:.0018,recover:.052,recoilPitch:.006,recoilYaw:.0065},
      rifle:{base:.00145,move:.036,air:.092,spray:.0022,recover:.04,recoilPitch:.0095,recoilYaw:.008},
      shotgun:{base:.004,move:.022,air:.07,spray:.001,recover:.065,recoilPitch:.018,recoilYaw:.01},
      sniper:{base:.00045,move:.095,air:.17,spray:.0035,recover:.028,recoilPitch:.02,recoilYaw:.009},
      heavy:{base:.011,move:.04,air:.11,spray:.0028,recover:.025,recoilPitch:.007,recoilYaw:.012},
      melee:{base:0,move:0,air:0,spray:0,recover:1,recoilPitch:0,recoilYaw:0}
    };
    function accuracyClassFor(w){if(w.type==='melee')return'melee';if(w.sniper)return'sniper';if(w.id==='negev')return'heavy';if(w.type==='shotgun')return'shotgun';if(w.category==='Pistols')return'pistol';if(w.category==='Mid-Tier')return'smg';return'rifle';}
    for(const w of weapons){Object.assign(w,ACCURACY_CLASS[accuracyClassFor(w)]);}

    const SLOT_PRIMARY=0,SLOT_SECONDARY=1,SLOT_KNIFE=2;
    const SLOT_LABELS=['PRIMARY','SECONDARY','KNIFE'];
    // Valve Arms Race uses a randomized weapon list while keeping a fixed
    // category order. CatStrike uses every matching weapon already present in
    // this single-file game: 3 SMGs -> 4 rifles -> 2 shotguns -> 2 snipers ->
    // 1 machine gun -> 4 pistols -> Golden Paw.
    const GUN_GAME_CATEGORY_SEQUENCE=Object.freeze([
      Object.freeze({label:'SMG',count:3,ids:Object.freeze(['mp9','mac10','ump45','p90'])}),
      Object.freeze({label:'RIFLE',count:4,ids:Object.freeze(['famas','galil','m4a4','m4a1s','ak47','aug','sg553'])}),
      Object.freeze({label:'SHOTGUN',count:2,ids:Object.freeze(['nova','xm1014','mag7'])}),
      // AWP is mandatory. The second sniper remains randomized so each match
      // still has Valve-style category variation without ever omitting the AWP.
      Object.freeze({label:'SNIPER',count:2,required:'awp',ids:Object.freeze(['ssg08','awp','scar20'])}),
      Object.freeze({label:'MACHINE GUN',count:1,ids:Object.freeze(['negev'])}),
      Object.freeze({label:'PISTOL',count:4,ids:Object.freeze(['usp','glock','p250','deagle'])})
    ]);
    const GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS=[];
    const gunGameShuffleScratch=[];
    function appendRandomGunGameCategory(ids,count,skipId=''){
      let length=0;
      for(let i=0;i<ids.length;i++)if(ids[i]!==skipId)gunGameShuffleScratch[length++]=ids[i];
      gunGameShuffleScratch.length=length;
      for(let i=length-1;i>0;i--){
        const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));
        const temp=gunGameShuffleScratch[i];gunGameShuffleScratch[i]=gunGameShuffleScratch[j];gunGameShuffleScratch[j]=temp;
      }
      for(let i=0;i<count&&i<length;i++)GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.push(gunGameShuffleScratch[i]);
    }
    function buildGunGameLadder(){
      GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length=0;
      for(const category of GUN_GAME_CATEGORY_SEQUENCE){
        if(category.required){
          GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.push(category.required);
          appendRandomGunGameCategory(category.ids,category.count-1,category.required);
        }else appendRandomGunGameCategory(category.ids,category.count);
      }
      GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.push('goldenpaw');
      return GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS;
    }
    function gunGameCategoryForId(id){
      if(id==='goldenpaw')return'FINAL';
      for(const category of GUN_GAME_CATEGORY_SEQUENCE)if(category.ids.includes(id))return category.label;
      return'WEAPON';
    }

    const defaultPrimaryForTeam=team=>team==='meowfia'?'ak47':'m4a4';

    let shotsFired=0,accuracyPenalty=0,currentInaccuracy=0;

    // Persistent input settings. Defaults exactly reproduce R180 sensitivity.
    const CATSTRIKE_SETTINGS_KEY='catstrike.settings.v1';
    const DEFAULT_INPUT_SETTINGS=Object.freeze({
      mouse:1,mouseAds:.00138/.00225,touch:1,touchAds:.0027/.0046,invertY:false
    });
    const inputSettings={...DEFAULT_INPUT_SETTINGS};
    const clampSetting=(value,min,max,fallback)=>{value=Number(value);return Number.isFinite(value)?Math.max(min,Math.min(max,value)):fallback;};
    try{
      const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CATSTRIKE_SETTINGS_KEY)||'null');
      if(saved&&typeof saved==='object'){
        inputSettings.mouse=clampSetting(saved.mouse,.2,3,DEFAULT_INPUT_SETTINGS.mouse);
        inputSettings.mouseAds=clampSetting(saved.mouseAds,.2,1.2,DEFAULT_INPUT_SETTINGS.mouseAds);
        inputSettings.touch=clampSetting(saved.touch,.2,3,DEFAULT_INPUT_SETTINGS.touch);
        inputSettings.touchAds=clampSetting(saved.touchAds,.2,1.2,DEFAULT_INPUT_SETTINGS.touchAds);
        inputSettings.invertY=Boolean(saved.invertY);
      }
    }catch(_){}
    const BASE_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY=.00225,BASE_TOUCH_SENSITIVITY=.0046,BASE_CURSOR_STEER_SPEED=2.9;
    let activeMouseSensitivity=BASE_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY,activeMousePitchScale=-BASE_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY;
    let activeTouchSensitivity=BASE_TOUCH_SENSITIVITY,activeTouchPitchScale=-BASE_TOUCH_SENSITIVITY;
    let activeCursorSteerSpeed=BASE_CURSOR_STEER_SPEED,activeCursorPitchScale=-BASE_CURSOR_STEER_SPEED*.7;
    function refreshInputSensitivity(){
      const mouseAds=aiming?inputSettings.mouseAds:1,touchAds=aiming?inputSettings.touchAds:1,pitchSign=inputSettings.invertY?1:-1;
      activeMouseSensitivity=BASE_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY*inputSettings.mouse*mouseAds;
      activeMousePitchScale=activeMouseSensitivity*pitchSign;
      activeTouchSensitivity=BASE_TOUCH_SENSITIVITY*inputSettings.touch*touchAds;
      activeTouchPitchScale=activeTouchSensitivity*pitchSign;
      // Cursor-steer was not ADS-scaled in R180; preserve that behavior exactly.
      activeCursorSteerSpeed=BASE_CURSOR_STEER_SPEED*inputSettings.mouse;
      activeCursorPitchScale=activeCursorSteerSpeed*.7*pitchSign;
    }
    function persistInputSettings(){try{localStorage.setItem(CATSTRIKE_SETTINGS_KEY,JSON.stringify(inputSettings));}catch(_){}}
    refreshInputSensitivity();
    const RECOIL_YAW_PATTERN=[0,-.45,.3,-.7,.55,-.2,.8,-.6,.35,-.9,.65,-.35,.95,-.75,.45];
    function resetWeaponAccuracy(){shotsFired=0;accuracyPenalty=0;currentInaccuracy=0;}
    function calculateInaccuracy(w){
      if(w.type==='melee')return 0;
      const speedRatio=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(player.moveSpeed/5.6,0,1.25);
      const moveFactor=Math.max(0,(speedRatio-.06)/.94);
      let value=w.base+w.move*moveFactor*moveFactor;
      if(!player.onGround)value+=w.air;
      if(player.crouched&&player.onGround)value*=.72;
      if(aiming){value*=w.sniper ? .26 : .82;}
      if(w.sniper&&!aiming)value+=.028;
      if(w.id==='negev'){
        const settled=THREE.MathUtils.clamp((shotsFired-7)/14,0,1);
        value+=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(accuracyPenalty+.022,.0025,settled);
      }else value+=accuracyPenalty;
      return THREE.MathUtils.clamp(value,0,.22);
    }
    function applyShotRecoil(w){
      const idx=Math.min(shotsFired,RECOIL_YAW_PATTERN.length-1);
      const growth=1+Math.min(shotsFired,14)*.055;
      player.pitch=Math.min(1.22,player.pitch+w.recoilPitch*growth);
      player.yaw+=RECOIL_YAW_PATTERN[idx]*w.recoilYaw*growth;
    }
    function setAiming(value) {
      const next=Boolean(value&&started&&!paused&&!gameOver&&!player.dead&&!buyMenuOpen);
      if(aiming===next)return;
      aiming=next;refreshInputSensitivity();
      playActionSfx(aiming?'scopeOn':'scopeOff');
      document.body.classList.toggle('aiming',aiming);
      if (mobileScopeButton) { mobileScopeButton.classList.toggle('active',aiming); mobileScopeButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(aiming)); }
    }
    function toggleAiming() {
      if(started&&!paused&&!gameOver&&!player.dead&&!buyMenuOpen)cancelRespawnProtection();
      setAiming(!aiming);
    }
    let started=false,paused=false,gameOver=false;
    let timeLeft=300,lastTime=performance.now(),respawnAt=0;

    // CS2-style respawn protection for respawn modes: ten seconds maximum,
    // cancelled immediately by a meaningful gameplay action. Looking around and
    // menu/pause interaction stay safe so players can orient themselves first.
    const RESPAWN_PROTECTION_SECONDS=10;
    let respawnProtectionArmed=false;
    const RESPAWN_CANCEL_KEY_CODES=new Set(['KeyW','KeyA','KeyS','KeyD','KeyR','KeyE','ControlLeft','ControlRight','Digit1','Digit2','Digit3']);
    function cancelRespawnProtection(){
      if(!respawnProtectionArmed)return false;
      respawnProtectionArmed=false;player.invulnerableUntil=0;return true;
    }
    function armRespawnProtection(now=performance.now()/1000){
      if(activeMode.respawn){
        respawnProtectionArmed=true;player.invulnerableUntil=now+RESPAWN_PROTECTION_SECONDS;
      }else{
        respawnProtectionArmed=false;player.invulnerableUntil=now+(activeMode.roundBased?.35:1.1);
      }
    }

    const gunPivot = new THREE.Group();
    gunPivot.position.set(.55,-.48,-1.25);
    camera.add(gunPivot);
    const gunCats=weapons.map(w=>{
      const c=createGunCat(w.fur,w.renderType||w.type);
      c.visible=false;
      const fadeMaterials=[];
      c.traverse(node=>{
        if(!node.isMesh||!node.material)return;
        const mats=Array.isArray(node.material)?node.material:[node.material];
        const clones=mats.map(mat=>{const clone=mat.clone();clone.transparent=true;clone.opacity=1;clone.depthWrite=true;fadeMaterials.push(clone);return clone;});
        node.material=Array.isArray(node.material)?clones:clones[0];
      });
      c.userData.fadeMaterials=fadeMaterials;
      gunPivot.add(c);return c;
    });
    gunCats[weaponIndex].visible=true;
    const scopeOverlayElement = $('scopeOverlay');
    const crosshairElement = $('crosshair');
    let lastScopeOpacity = '';
    let lastScopeSniper = null;
    let lastCrosshairOpacity = '';
    let lastCrosshairTransform = '', lastCrosshairScale = NaN;
    function setCurrentGunOpacity(value){
      const gun=gunCats[weaponIndex];if(!gun)return;
      const opacity=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(value,0,1),depthWrite=opacity>.72;
      // ADS spends most gameplay frames fully settled at exactly 0 or 1. Avoid
      // rewriting every cloned weapon material when neither render property has
      // changed; transition frames still update at the exact original cadence.
      if(gun.userData.fadeOpacity===opacity&&gun.userData.fadeDepthWrite===depthWrite)return;
      for(const mat of gun.userData.fadeMaterials||[]){mat.opacity=opacity;mat.depthWrite=depthWrite;}
      gun.userData.fadeOpacity=opacity;gun.userData.fadeDepthWrite=depthWrite;
    }
    function forceClearScope(){
      adsProgress=0;setCurrentGunOpacity(1);
      if(scopeOverlayElement && lastScopeOpacity!=='0'){scopeOverlayElement.style.opacity='0';lastScopeOpacity='0';}
      if(crosshairElement && lastCrosshairOpacity!=='1'){crosshairElement.style.opacity='1';lastCrosshairOpacity='1';}
    }

    const muzzleFlash = mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(.12,0), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xffe08b, transparent:true, opacity:0}), false, false);
    muzzleFlash.position.set(0,-.02,-1.62);
    gunPivot.add(muzzleFlash);
    const muzzleLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffbf62, 0, 5.5, 2);
    muzzleLight.position.copy(muzzleFlash.position); gunPivot.add(muzzleLight);
    gunPivot.traverse(node => { if (node.isMesh) node.layers.set(MODEL_OUTLINE_DYNAMIC_OCCLUDER_LAYER); });

    // ---------- Input ----------
    const INPUT_W=1,INPUT_S=2,INPUT_A=4,INPUT_D=8,INPUT_CTRL_L=16,INPUT_CTRL_R=32,INPUT_SHIFT_L=64,INPUT_SHIFT_R=128,INPUT_USE=256;
    const INPUT_CODE_BITS=Object.freeze({KeyW:INPUT_W,KeyS:INPUT_S,KeyA:INPUT_A,KeyD:INPUT_D,ControlLeft:INPUT_CTRL_L,ControlRight:INPUT_CTRL_R,ShiftLeft:INPUT_SHIFT_L,ShiftRight:INPUT_SHIFT_R,KeyE:INPUT_USE});
    let inputKeyMask=0,inputMoveX=0,inputMoveZ=0,inputCrouch=false,inputWalking=false,inputUseHeld=false;
    function refreshDerivedInputState(){
      const mask=inputKeyMask;
      inputMoveX=((mask&INPUT_D)?1:0)-((mask&INPUT_A)?1:0);
      inputMoveZ=((mask&INPUT_S)?1:0)-((mask&INPUT_W)?1:0);
      inputCrouch=Boolean(mask&(INPUT_CTRL_L|INPUT_CTRL_R));
      inputWalking=Boolean(mask&(INPUT_SHIFT_L|INPUT_SHIFT_R));
      inputUseHeld=Boolean(mask&INPUT_USE);
    }
    function setInputKey(code,down){
      const bit=INPUT_CODE_BITS[code]||0;if(!bit)return;
      const nextMask=down?(inputKeyMask|bit):(inputKeyMask&~bit);if(nextMask===inputKeyMask)return;
      inputKeyMask=nextMask;refreshDerivedInputState();
    }
    function clearInputKeys(){
      if(!inputKeyMask&&!inputMoveX&&!inputMoveZ&&!inputCrouch&&!inputWalking&&!inputUseHeld)return;
      inputKeyMask=0;inputMoveX=inputMoveZ=0;inputCrouch=inputWalking=inputUseHeld=false;
    }
    const mobileScopeButton=$('scopeBtn');
    addEventListener('keydown',(e)=>{
      setInputKey(e.code,true);
      if(respawnProtectionArmed&&started&&!paused&&!gameOver&&!player.dead&&!buyMenuOpen&&RESPAWN_CANCEL_KEY_CODES.has(e.code))cancelRespawnProtection();
      if(e.code==='KeyR')startReload();
      if(e.code==='KeyB'&&!e.repeat){e.preventDefault();toggleBuyMenu(e);}
      if(e.code==='Digit1')switchOwnedSlot(0);
      if(e.code==='Digit2')switchOwnedSlot(1);
      if(e.code==='Digit3')switchOwnedSlot(2);
      if(e.code==='Space'){e.preventDefault();jump();}
      if(e.code==='Escape'&&settingsMenu.classList.contains('open')){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();closeSettings();return;}
      if(e.code==='Escape'&&started&&!gameOver){if(buyMenuOpen)closeBuyMenu(e);else setPause(true);}
    });
    addEventListener('keyup', e => setInputKey(e.code,false));

    let lockRequestPending = false;
    let lockRequestToken = 0;
    let pointerLockWasActive = false;
    let pointerLockedToGame = false;
    let explicitPointerUnlock = false;

    function activePointerLockElement() {
      return document.pointerLockElement || document.mozPointerLockElement || document.webkitPointerLockElement || null;
    }

    function pointerLockSupported() {
      return Boolean(
        renderer.domElement.requestPointerLock ||
        renderer.domElement.mozRequestPointerLock ||
        renderer.domElement.webkitRequestPointerLock
      );
    }

    let dragLookMode = false;        // fallback aiming when lock is impossible
    let dragLooking = false;

    function lockDiagnostics(err) {
      const embedded = window.top !== window.self;
      const lines = [];
      lines.push('DIAGNOSTICS — why mouse lock failed:');
      lines.push('• Ewwow: ' + ((err && err.name) ? (err.name + ': ' + err.message) : 'no detaiw (event-based wejection)'));
      lines.push('• URL scheme: ' + location.protocol + ((location.protocol === 'about:' || location.protocol === 'data:' || location.protocol === 'blob:') ? ' ← lock is often blocked on this scheme' : ''));
      lines.push('• Embedded in iframe: ' + (embedded ? 'YES ← the parent iframe must grant allow="pointer-lock"; without it, lock can NEVER work here' : 'no'));
      lines.push('• Document focused: ' + (document.hasFocus() ? 'yes' : 'NO ← click the page once first'));
      if (document.featurePolicy && typeof document.featurePolicy.allowsFeature === 'function') {
        try { lines.push('• Permissions-Policy allows pointer-lock: ' + (document.featurePolicy.allowsFeature('pointer-lock') ? 'yes' : 'NO ← bwocked by page/iframe powicy; unfixable in code')); } catch(_){}
      }
      lines.push('');
      lines.push(embedded
        ? 'FIX: open this HTML in its own browser tab, because the parent iframe must explicitly allow pointer lock.'
        : 'FIX: click DEPLOY or RESUME again. If the browser rejects pointer lock, cursor-steer fallback activates automatically.');
      return lines.join('\n');
    }

    function nativeRequestPointerLock() {
      const element = renderer.domElement;
      const fn = element.requestPointerLock || element.mozRequestPointerLock || element.webkitRequestPointerLock;
      if (!fn) throw new Error('Pointer Lock API unavailable');
      // Use the plain, broadly-supported request. Requesting raw/unadjusted input
      // can trigger extra permission handling or inconsistent promise/event timing.
      return fn.call(element);
    }

    function enterGameplay() {
      const wasPaused=paused;paused = false;
      if(wasPaused)playActionSfx('pauseClose');
      updateAmbientAudio(performance.now()*.001,true);
      document.body.classList.remove('game-paused');
      if (usingMobileControls() && started && !gameOver) document.body.classList.add('mobile-controls-active');
      else document.body.classList.remove('mobile-controls-active');
      pauseScreen.style.display = 'none';
      lastTime = performance.now();wakeAnimationLoop();
      try { renderer.domElement.focus({preventScroll:true}); }
      catch (_) { try { renderer.domElement.focus(); } catch (_) {} }
    }

    function enableDragLookFallback() {
      if (dragLookMode) return;
      dragLookMode = true;

      paused = false;
      pauseScreen.style.display = 'none';
      lastTime = performance.now();wakeAnimationLoop();
      renderer.domElement.style.cursor = 'crosshair';
      const hint = document.createElement('div');
      hint.textContent = 'NO-LOCK AIM: move cursor toward screen edges to turn · LEFT click to fire · P to pause';
      hint.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:8px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);background:rgba(0,0,0,.75);color:#ffd24a;padding:7px 16px;border-radius:8px;font:600 12px system-ui;z-index:50;pointer-events:none';
      document.body.appendChild(hint);
      setTimeout(() => hint.remove(), 8000);
    }
    // Cursor-steer aim state: the camera turns when the cursor leaves a central
    // deadzone, faster the closer it gets to the screen edge. No lock required.
    const steer = { x: 0, y: 0 };
    let pointerCoordScaleX=2/Math.max(1,innerWidth),pointerCoordScaleY=2/Math.max(1,innerHeight);
    addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
      steer.x = e.clientX*pointerCoordScaleX-1;   // -1..1
      steer.y = e.clientY*pointerCoordScaleY-1;
    });
    function applyCursorSteer(dt) {
      if (!dragLookMode || paused || !started || gameOver) return;
      const dead=.22,invLive=1/(1-dead),rawX=steer.x,rawY=steer.y,absX=Math.abs(rawX),absY=Math.abs(rawY);
      const sx=absX>dead?(rawX>0?(rawX-dead)*invLive:(rawX+dead)*invLive):0;
      const sy=absY>dead?(rawY>0?(rawY-dead)*invLive:(rawY+dead)*invLive):0;
      player.yaw -= sx*Math.abs(sx)*activeCursorSteerSpeed*dt;
      player.pitch += sy*Math.abs(sy)*activeCursorPitchScale*dt;
      player.pitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(player.pitch, -1.22, 1.22);
    }
    addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.code === 'KeyP' && dragLookMode && started && !gameOver) {
        paused = !paused;playActionSfx(paused?'pauseOpen':'pauseClose');updateAmbientAudio(performance.now()*.001,true);
        pauseScreen.style.display = paused ? 'grid' : 'none';
        if (!paused) lastTime = performance.now();
      }
    });

    function requestMouseLock(event) {
      // Keep the native request inside the trusted Play/Resume click, but never
      // freeze simulation while waiting for browser pointer-lock bookkeeping.
      if (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
      }
      if (!started || gameOver) return;
      if (activePointerLockElement()) {
        enterGameplay();
        return;
      }

      ensureAudio();
      firing = false;
      clearInputKeys();
      enterGameplay();

      if (!pointerLockSupported()) {
        enableDragLookFallback();
        return;
      }
      if (lockRequestPending) return;

      lockRequestPending = true;
      const token = ++lockRequestToken;
      const failToFallback = (err) => {
        if (token !== lockRequestToken || activePointerLockElement() || gameOver || !started) return;
        lockRequestPending = false;
        if (err) safeConsoleWarn('CatStrike:', lockDiagnostics(err));
        enableDragLookFallback();
      };

      try {
        const request = nativeRequestPointerLock();
        if (request && typeof request.then === 'function') {
          request.then(() => {
            if (token !== lockRequestToken) return;
            lockRequestPending = false;
            if (activePointerLockElement()) enterGameplay();
          }).catch(failToFallback);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        failToFallback(error);
        return;
      }

      // Older browsers return no Promise and some embedded viewers swallow the
      // error event. A short state check guarantees the game remains playable.
      setTimeout(() => failToFallback(null), 700);
    }

    function beginPrimaryFire() {
      if (!started || gameOver || paused) return;
      if (!dragLookMode && !pointerLockedToGame) return;
      primeSfxContext();
      firing = true;
      fireWeapon(performance.now()/1000);
    }

    // Capture physical mouse buttons at the document level. Pointer Lock can
    // retarget mouse events differently across browsers and embedded viewers;
    // this path remains reliable while still ignoring menu/shop clicks.
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
      if (e.sourceCapabilities?.firesTouchEvents) return;
      physicalMouseDetected = true;
      if (!started || gameOver) return;
      const lockedToGame = pointerLockedToGame;
      const onGameCanvas = e.target === renderer.domElement;
      if (!lockedToGame && !onGameCanvas) return;

      if (e.button === 2) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!paused) toggleAiming();
        return;
      }
      if (e.button === 0) beginPrimaryFire();
      if (dragLookMode && e.button === 1 && !paused) dragLooking = true;
    }, {capture:true});
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (!started || gameOver) return;
      if (!pointerLockedToGame) {
        requestMouseLock(e);   // always retryable — succeeding upgrades out of fallback
      }
    });
    addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
      if (e.button === 0) firing = false;
      if (e.button === 1) dragLooking = false;
